} V;\n\nstatic inline V vsub(V a, V b) { return (V){a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z}; }\nstatic inline V vadd(V a, V b) { return (V){a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z}; }\nstatic inline V vmul(V a, float s) { return (V){a.x*s, a.y*s, a.z*s}; }\nstatic inline float vdot(V a, V b) { return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z; }\nstatic inline V vnorm(V a) { float l = sqrtf(vdot(a, a)); return (V){a.x/l, a.y/l, a.z/l}; }\nstatic inline V vc(V x, V y, V z) { return (V){x.x, y.y, z.z}; }\n\ntypedef struct {\n    V o, d;\n    V hit_p, hit_n;\n    int hit;\n    int hit_type; // 0=none, 1=sphere, 2=plane\n} Hit;\n\nstatic V sphere_c = {-0.5f, -0.5f, -5.0f};\nstatic float sphere_r = 1.0f;\n\nstatic int sphere_intersect(V o, V d, Hit *h) {\n    V oc = vsub(o, sphere_c);\n    float a = vdot(d, d);\n    float b = 2.0f * vdot(d, oc);\n    float c = vdot(oc, oc) - sphere_r * sphere_r;\n    float disc = b*b - 4*a*c;\n    if (disc < 0) return 0;\n    float t = (-b - sqrtf(disc)) / (2*a);\n    if (t < 0.001f) return 0;\n    h->hit_p = vadd(o, vmul(d, t));\n    h->hit_n = vnorm(vsub(h->hit_p, sphere_c));\n    h->hit = 1;\n    h->hit_type = 1;\n    return 1;\n}\n\nstatic int plane_intersect(V o, V d, float y_plane, Hit *h) {\n    if (fabsf(d.y) < 0.001f) return 0;\n    float t = (y_plane - o.y) / d.y;\n    if (t < 0.001f) return 0;\n    h->hit_p = vadd(o, vmul(d, t));\n    h->hit_n = (V){0, 1, 0};\n    h->hit = 1;\n    h->hit_type = 2;\n    return 1;\n}\n\nstatic V sky_color(V d) {\n    float t = 0.5f * (d.y + 1);\n    V white = {1, 1, 1};\n    V blue = {0.5, 0.7, 1};\n    V c1 = vmul(white, 1-t);\n    V c2 = vmul(blue, t);\n    return vadd(c1, c2);\n}\n\nint main() {\n    fprintf(stderr, \"Creating high-resolution image: %dx%d\\n\", W, H);\n    fprintf(stderr, \"This will be downsampled with ffmpeg after rendering\\n\");\n    \n    V cam_dir = vnorm((V){1, 1, -1});\n    fprintf(stderr, \"Rendering high-resolution image...\\n\");\n    \n    V **image = malloc(H * sizeof(V*));\n    for (int i = 0; i < H; i++) image[i] = malloc(W * sizeof(V));\n    \n    V light_dir = vnorm((V){-1, 1, -1}); // light from above-right\n    \n    for (int y = 0; y < H; y++) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Progress: %.1f%%\\r\", 100.0 * y / (H-1));\n        for (int x = 0; x < W; x++) {\n            // Compute ray\n            float u = (8.0f/3.0f) * ((float)x / (W-1)) - 1.0f;\n            float v = 2.0f * (1.0f - (float)y / (H-1));\n            \n            V d = vnorm(vadd(vadd(vmul((V){1,0,0}, u), vmul((V){0,1,0}, v)), cam_dir));\n            \n            Hit h = {0};\n            int hit = sphere_intersect((V){0,0,0}, d, &h);\n            if (!hit) hit = plane_intersect((V){0,0,0}, d, -1.5f, &h);\n            \n            V color;\n            if (!hit) {\n                color = sky_color(d);\n            } else {\n                // Diffuse lighting\n                float diffuse = fmaxf(0.0f, vdot(h.hit_n, light_dir));\n                float lighting = diffuse * 0.8f + 0.5f;\n                \n                // Shadow ray\n                V shadow_d = vnorm(vsub((V){-0.5f, 0, 0}, h.hit_p));
